# gift.ceo — scrisorile 152–233, băuturi

Continuarea lui `crypto/lot3-scrisori.md`, care ține 102–151.
Expeditor: **gift.ceo.support@gmail.com** · Contor la scriere: **8 din 10 libere**

82 de firme de băuturi alcoolice și nealcoolice. Adresele sunt citite de
pe situl fiecăreia, majoritatea din Impressum.

## De ce categoria asta e cea mai bună de până acum

**Randamentul căutării a fost 70%** — față de 10% la crypto și 13% la companiile
cotate. Firmele de familie își publică adresa; corporațiile o ascund după
formular.

**Și potrivirea e mai bună.** Un director angajat al unei companii cotate nu
poate dărui: ține în custodie proprietatea altora, iar a da un activ fără decizie
de consiliu e încălcare a obligației fiduciare. Un proprietar de berărie poate
decide și gata.

## Cinci argumente, după tipul firmei

| Tip | Argumentul |
|---|---|
| Trappist | Obligația de a dărui e în definiția produsului. |
| Public | Proprietarul e statul sau mănăstirea. Profitul iese deja. |
| Social | Firma a fost construită ca să dăruiască. |
| Familie | Ești unul dintre puținii care chiar *pot* dărui. |
| Izvor | Vinzi ceva ce cade din cer și e al tuturor până îl îmbuteliezi. |

## Onestitatea listei

**24 au un fapt verificabil** despre ce a dăruit firma — scris în primul paragraf.

**58 nu au.** Acolo scrisoarea spune adevărul în loc să inventeze:
*„I am writing without knowing what your house has given away, and that is the
honest reason for the letter rather than an apology for it."*

O deschidere onestă e mai bună decât un compliment fabricat. Un proprietar de
berărie de familie recunoaște instant lauda inventată.

---

# Trappist — obligația de a dărui e în definiția produsului

Regula appellation-ului: profitul acoperă nevoile comunității, restul se duce la caritate. Nu o promisiune — o condiție de a avea dreptul la nume.

---

**Subiect:** Ten founding seats on gift.ceo — yours costs nothing

> For Chimay.
>
> The monks of Scourmont have brewed since 1862 and the rule has not changed: what the community does not need goes out. You are one of a handful of businesses on earth whose right to use its own name depends on giving the money away.

[**Deschide în Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=info%40chimay.com&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Chimay.%0A%0AThe%20monks%20of%20Scourmont%20have%20brewed%20since%201862%20and%20the%20rule%20has%20not%20changed%3A%20what%20the%20community%20does%20not%20need%20goes%20out.%20You%20are%20one%20of%20a%20handful%20of%20businesses%20on%20earth%20whose%20right%20to%20use%20its%20own%20name%20depends%20on%20giving%20the%20money%20away.%0A%0AYour%20order%20answered%20the%20question%20in%20this%20letter%20about%20eight%20hundred%20years%20before%20it%20was%20asked.%0A%0AThere%20is%20no%20other%20industry%20on%20earth%20where%20the%20definition%20of%20the%20product%20contains%20an%20obligation%20to%20give%20the%20money%20away.%20Not%20a%20pledge%2C%20not%20a%20percentage%2C%20not%20a%20foundation%20set%20up%20afterwards%20%E2%80%94%20a%20condition%20of%20being%20allowed%20to%20use%20your%20own%20name.%20Every%20technology%20company%20now%20writing%20a%20values%20page%20is%20describing%2C%20badly%2C%20something%20your%20order%20simply%20does.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%20anyway.%0A%0AYou%20have%20given%20for%20centuries%20and%20never%20once%20been%20listed%20anywhere%20a%20chief%20executive%20would%20look.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20under%20the%20name%20of%20the%20person%20responsible.%20Your%20entry%20would%20sit%20next%20to%20software%20companies%20and%20chip%20manufacturers%2C%20and%20it%20would%20be%20older%20than%20all%20of%20them%20by%20four%20hundred%20years.%20That%20is%20the%20point%3A%20to%20put%20in%20front%20of%20the%20people%20who%20have%20given%20nothing%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20who%20did%20are%20named.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Chimay%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

> For Westmalle.
>
> You invented the Tripel and never trademarked it. Every brewery in the world now makes one, uses the word freely, and owes you nothing — because you let a style you created become common property. Then there is the Trappist rule itself, which sends the surplus out by obligation rather than choice.

# Proprietate publică sau monahală

Profitul nu se duce la acționari, fiindcă nu există. Statul, sau mănăstirea.

| | |
|---|---|
| Către | Badische Staatsbrauerei Rothaus |
| Adresa | info@rothaus.de |
| Unde | rothaus.de/impressum |

**Subiect:** Ten founding seats on gift.ceo — yours costs nothing

> For Rothaus.
>
> The brewery belongs to the state of Baden-Württemberg. Every mark of profit from Tannenzäpfle has gone to the public purse since 1806 — schools, roads, the ordinary business of a state — and the people drinking it in Berlin mostly have no idea. It is a company that has been giving its entire output away for two centuries by construction.

[**Deschide în Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=info%40rothaus.de&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Rothaus.%0A%0AThe%20brewery%20belongs%20to%20the%20state%20of%20Baden-W%C3%BCrttemberg.%20Every%20mark%20of%20profit%20from%20Tannenz%C3%A4pfle%20has%20gone%20to%20the%20public%20purse%20since%201806%20%E2%80%94%20schools%2C%20roads%2C%20the%20ordinary%20business%20of%20a%20state%20%E2%80%94%20and%20the%20people%20drinking%20it%20in%20Berlin%20mostly%20have%20no%20idea.%20It%20is%20a%20company%20that%20has%20been%20giving%20its%20entire%20output%20away%20for%20two%20centuries%20by%20construction.%0A%0AThere%20is%20a%20whole%20category%20of%20company%20that%20gives%20by%20construction%2C%20and%20nobody%20has%20ever%20counted%20it.%0A%0ABreweries%20owned%20by%20states%2C%20monasteries%20and%20foundations%20have%20been%20sending%20their%20surplus%20outward%20for%20centuries%20without%20once%20calling%20it%20philanthropy%2C%20because%20it%20is%20not%20a%20programme%20%E2%80%94%20it%20is%20simply%20how%20they%20were%20built.%20It%20is%20the%20oldest%20form%20of%20the%20thing%20this%20letter%20is%20about%2C%20and%20there%20is%20no%20list%20of%20it%20anywhere.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20arrangement%20you%20operate%20under%20is%20a%20gift%20that%20nobody%20records%20as%20one.%0A%0AA%20page%20that%20numbers%20gifts%20and%20names%20the%20person%20responsible%20is%20where%20that%20belongs%20%E2%80%94%20not%20in%20a%20corporate%20history%20nobody%20reads%2C%20but%20next%20to%20the%20companies%20that%20have%20just%20discovered%20generosity%20and%20put%20out%20a%20press%20release%20about%20it.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Rothaus%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20arrangement%20you%20operate%20under%20is%20a%20gift%20that%20nobody%20records%20as%20one.%0A%0AA%20page%20that%20numbers%20gifts%20and%20names%20the%20person%20responsible%20is%20where%20that%20belongs%20%E2%80%94%20not%20in%20a%20corporate%20history%20nobody%20reads%2C%20but%20next%20to%20the%20companies%20that%20have%20just%20discovered%20generosity%20and%20put%20out%20a%20press%20release%20about%20it.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Weihenstephan%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

Dăruitul nu e un program atașat afacerii, e motivul pentru care afacerea a fost desenată așa.

## 158. Lemonaid

| | |
|---|---|
| Către | Lemonaid Beverages GmbH |
| Adresa | info@lemonaid.de |
| Unde | lemon-aid.de/impressum |

**Subiect:** Ten founding seats on gift.ceo — yours costs nothing

> For Lemonaid.
>
> Five cents of every bottle goes out to the growing regions through your own association, and you publish what it funded. You also fought the German authorities who wanted you to add sugar to be legally allowed to call it lemonade, and the rules changed. Winning an argument that makes a product category healthier for everyone, competitors included, is a gift that never gets counted as one.

[**Deschide în Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=info%40lemonaid.de&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Lemonaid.%0A%0AFive%20cents%20of%20every%20bottle%20goes%20out%20to%20the%20growing%20regions%20through%20your%20own%20association%2C%20and%20you%20publish%20what%20it%20funded.%20You%20also%20fought%20the%20German%20authorities%20who%20wanted%20you%20to%20add%20sugar%20to%20be%20legally%20allowed%20to%20call%20it%20lemonade%2C%20and%20the%20rules%20changed.%20Winning%20an%20argument%20that%20makes%20a%20product%20category%20healthier%20for%20everyone%2C%20competitors%20included%2C%20is%20a%20gift%20that%20never%20gets%20counted%20as%20one.%0A%0AYour%20company%20was%20built%20to%20give%2C%20which%20makes%20this%20letter%20unusual.%0A%0AMost%20of%20the%20people%20I%20write%20to%20have%20to%20find%20the%20thing%20they%20gave.%20You%20started%20from%20it%3A%20the%20giving%20is%20not%20a%20programme%20attached%20to%20the%20business%2C%20it%20is%20the%20reason%20the%20business%20was%20drawn%20up%20that%20way.%20What%20you%20are%20missing%20is%20not%20generosity%2C%20it%20is%20a%20place%20to%20put%20it%20where%20it%20is%20counted%20next%20to%20everyone%20else's.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AYou%20are%20read%20as%20a%20marketing%20position%2C%20and%20you%20cannot%20argue%20your%20way%20out%20of%20it.%0A%0AEvery%20company%20now%20claims%20a%20purpose%2C%20which%20has%20made%20the%20claim%20worthless%20%E2%80%94%20including%20yours%2C%20which%20happens%20to%20be%20true.%20The%20only%20thing%20that%20still%20separates%20a%20real%20one%20is%20a%20specific%20gift%20with%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date%20on%20it%2C%20sitting%20next%20to%20other%20specific%20gifts%2C%20where%20anyone%20can%20compare%20them.%20That%20is%20the%20whole%20of%20what%20gift.ceo%20is.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Lemonaid%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=presse%40bionade.de&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Bionade.%0A%0AA%20master%20brewer%20spent%20eight%20years%20and%20most%20of%20what%20he%20had%20inventing%20a%20fermented%20soft%20drink%20that%20did%20not%20exist%2C%20and%20nearly%20lost%20the%20family%20brewery%20doing%20it.%20The%20recipe%20changed%20what%20a%20whole%20category%20could%20be%2C%20and%20the%20people%20who%20copied%20it%20paid%20nothing.%0A%0AYour%20company%20was%20built%20to%20give%2C%20which%20makes%20this%20letter%20unusual.%0A%0AMost%20of%20the%20people%20I%20write%20to%20have%20to%20find%20the%20thing%20they%20gave.%20You%20started%20from%20it%3A%20the%20giving%20is%20not%20a%20programme%20attached%20to%20the%20business%2C%20it%20is%20the%20reason%20the%20business%20was%20drawn%20up%20that%20way.%20What%20you%20are%20missing%20is%20not%20generosity%2C%20it%20is%20a%20place%20to%20put%20it%20where%20it%20is%20counted%20next%20to%20everyone%20else's.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AYou%20are%20read%20as%20a%20marketing%20position%2C%20and%20you%20cannot%20argue%20your%20way%20out%20of%20it.%0A%0AEvery%20company%20now%20claims%20a%20purpose%2C%20which%20has%20made%20the%20claim%20worthless%20%E2%80%94%20including%20yours%2C%20which%20happens%20to%20be%20true.%20The%20only%20thing%20that%20still%20separates%20a%20real%20one%20is%20a%20specific%20gift%20with%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date%20on%20it%2C%20sitting%20next%20to%20other%20specific%20gifts%2C%20where%20anyone%20can%20compare%20them.%20That%20is%20the%20whole%20of%20what%20gift.ceo%20is.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Bionade%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

## 165. Neuzeller Klosterbrauerei

| | |
|---|---|
| Către | Stefan Fritsche |
| Adresa | fritsche@klosterbrauerei.com |
| Unde | klosterbrauerei.com/impressum |

**Subiect:** Ten founding seats on gift.ceo — yours costs nothing

> For Stefan Fritsche.
>
> You publish your own address on the brewery's imprint, which almost no managing director still does. You also fought the German state for years over whether a beer brewed with sugar could legally be called beer, and won — a ruling that widened what every other brewer in the country is allowed to make.

[**Deschide în Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=fritsche%40klosterbrauerei.com&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Stefan%20Fritsche.%0A%0AYou%20publish%20your%20own%20address%20on%20the%20brewery's%20imprint%2C%20which%20almost%20no%20managing%20director%20still%20does.%20You%20also%20fought%20the%20German%20state%20for%20years%20over%20whether%20a%20beer%20brewed%20with%20sugar%20could%20legally%20be%20called%20beer%2C%20and%20won%20%E2%80%94%20a%20ruling%20that%20widened%20what%20every%20other%20brewer%20in%20the%20country%20is%20allowed%20to%20make.%0A%0AYou%20are%20one%20of%20the%20few%20kinds%20of%20company%20that%20can%20actually%20give.%0A%0AA%20listed%20company%20cannot.%20A%20hired%20chief%20executive%20holds%20other%20people's%20property%20in%20trust%2C%20and%20handing%20an%20asset%20over%20without%20a%20board%20behind%20him%20is%20not%20generosity%20but%20a%20breach%20of%20duty.%20A%20family%20house%20that%20has%20held%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20no%20such%20problem%3A%20what%20you%20decide%20to%20give%20is%20yours%20to%20give.%20That%20is%20rarer%20than%20it%20sounds%20%E2%80%94%20most%20of%20the%20economy%20has%20lost%20the%20ability.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Neuzeller%20Klosterbrauerei%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

> Twenty-six generations, since 1385. A family that has held the same land for six hundred years measures obligation in a way a quarterly company cannot, and has almost certainly carried its region through years that are now only a line in a history book.

[**Deschide în Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=antinori%40antinori.it&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Marchesi%20Antinori.%0A%0ATwenty-six%20generations%2C%20since%201385.%20A%20family%20that%20has%20held%20the%20same%20land%20for%20six%20hundred%20years%20measures%20obligation%20in%20a%20way%20a%20quarterly%20company%20cannot%2C%20and%20has%20almost%20certainly%20carried%20its%20region%20through%20years%20that%20are%20now%20only%20a%20line%20in%20a%20history%20book.%0A%0AYou%20are%20one%20of%20the%20few%20kinds%20of%20company%20that%20can%20actually%20give.%0A%0AA%20listed%20company%20cannot.%20A%20hired%20chief%20executive%20holds%20other%20people's%20property%20in%20trust%2C%20and%20handing%20an%20asset%20over%20without%20a%20board%20behind%20him%20is%20not%20generosity%20but%20a%20breach%20of%20duty.%20A%20family%20house%20that%20has%20held%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20no%20such%20problem%3A%20what%20you%20decide%20to%20give%20is%20yours%20to%20give.%20That%20is%20rarer%20than%20it%20sounds%20%E2%80%94%20most%20of%20the%20economy%20has%20lost%20the%20ability.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Antinori%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=info%40brauereilocher.ch&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Brauerei%20Locher.%0A%0AFive%20generations%20in%20Appenzell%2C%20and%20you%20turned%20spent%20grain%20%E2%80%94%20the%20waste%20every%20brewery%20pays%20to%20remove%20%E2%80%94%20into%20food%20products%20and%20packaging%20material.%20Solving%20your%20own%20industry's%20waste%20problem%20and%20then%20selling%20the%20solution%20to%20your%20competitors%20is%20not%20the%20usual%20way%20to%20treat%20an%20advantage.%0A%0AYou%20are%20one%20of%20the%20few%20kinds%20of%20company%20that%20can%20actually%20give.%0A%0AA%20listed%20company%20cannot.%20A%20hired%20chief%20executive%20holds%20other%20people's%20property%20in%20trust%2C%20and%20handing%20an%20asset%20over%20without%20a%20board%20behind%20him%20is%20not%20generosity%20but%20a%20breach%20of%20duty.%20A%20family%20house%20that%20has%20held%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20no%20such%20problem%3A%20what%20you%20decide%20to%20give%20is%20yours%20to%20give.%20That%20is%20rarer%20than%20it%20sounds%20%E2%80%94%20most%20of%20the%20economy%20has%20lost%20the%20ability.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Locher%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

## 182. Bitburger

| | |
|---|---|
| Către | Bitburger Braugruppe |
| Adresa | info@bitburger.de |
| Unde | bitburger.de/impressum |

**Subiect:** Ten founding seats on gift.ceo — yours costs nothing

> For Bitburger.
>
> I am writing without knowing what your house has given away, and that is the honest reason for the letter rather than an apology for it. A brewery that has stood in the same town for generations has carried people through bad years — a wage kept paid, a roof fixed, beer at a funeral nobody was billed for. None of it was ever written down anywhere.

[**Deschide în Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=info%40bitburger.de&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Bitburger.%0A%0AI%20am%20writing%20without%20knowing%20what%20your%20house%20has%20given%20away%2C%20and%20that%20is%20the%20honest%20reason%20for%20the%20letter%20rather%20than%20an%20apology%20for%20it.%20A%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%2C%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%20nobody%20was%20billed%20for.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20ever%20written%20down%20anywhere.%0A%0AYou%20are%20one%20of%20the%20few%20kinds%20of%20company%20that%20can%20actually%20give.%0A%0AA%20listed%20company%20cannot.%20A%20hired%20chief%20executive%20holds%20other%20people's%20property%20in%20trust%2C%20and%20handing%20an%20asset%20over%20without%20a%20board%20behind%20him%20is%20not%20generosity%20but%20a%20breach%20of%20duty.%20A%20family%20house%20that%20has%20held%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20no%20such%20problem%3A%20what%20you%20decide%20to%20give%20is%20yours%20to%20give.%20That%20is%20rarer%20than%20it%20sounds%20%E2%80%94%20most%20of%20the%20economy%20has%20lost%20the%20ability.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Bitburger%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

0starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Gaffel%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

| | |
|---|---|
| Către | Tucher Bräu |
| Adresa | presse@tucher.de |
| Unde | tucher.de/impressum |

**Subiect:** Ten founding seats on gift.ceo — yours costs nothing

> For Tucher.
>
> I am writing without knowing what your house has given away, and that is the honest reason for the letter rather than an apology for it. A brewery that has stood in the same town for generations has carried people through bad years — a wage kept paid, a roof fixed, beer at a funeral nobody was billed for. None of it was ever written down anywhere.

[**Deschide în Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=presse%40tucher.de&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Tucher.%0A%0AI%20am%20writing%20without%20knowing%20what%20your%20house%20has%20given%20away%2C%20and%20that%20is%20the%20honest%20reason%20for%20the%20letter%20rather%20than%20an%20apology%20for%20it.%20A%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%2C%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%20nobody%20was%20billed%20for.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20ever%20written%20down%20anywhere.%0A%0AYou%20are%20one%20of%20the%20few%20kinds%20of%20company%20that%20can%20actually%20give.%0A%0AA%20listed%20company%20cannot.%20A%20hired%20chief%20executive%20holds%20other%20people's%20property%20in%20trust%2C%20and%20handing%20an%20asset%20over%20without%20a%20board%20behind%20him%20is%20not%20generosity%20but%20a%20breach%20of%20duty.%20A%20family%20house%20that%20has%20held%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20no%20such%20problem%3A%20what%20you%20decide%20to%20give%20is%20yours%20to%20give.%20That%20is%20rarer%20than%20it%20sounds%20%E2%80%94%20most%20of%20the%20economy%20has%20lost%20the%20ability.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Tucher%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

|---|---|

| Unde | schultheiss.de/impressum |

**Subiect:** Ten founding seats on gift.ceo — yours costs nothing

> For Schultheiss.
>
> I am writing without knowing what your house has given away, and that is the honest reason for the letter rather than an apology for it. A brewery that has stood in the same town for generations has carried people through bad years — a wage kept paid, a roof fixed, beer at a funeral nobody was billed for. None of it was ever written down anywhere.

[**Deschide în Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=info%40schultheiss.de&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Schultheiss.%0A%0AI%20am%20writing%20without%20knowing%20what%20your%20house%20has%20given%20away%2C%20and%20that%20is%20the%20honest%20reason%20for%20the%20letter%20rather%20than%20an%20apology%20for%20it.%20A%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%2C%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%20nobody%20was%20billed%20for.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20ever%20written%20down%20anywhere.%0A%0AYou%20are%20one%20of%20the%20few%20kinds%20of%20company%20that%20can%20actually%20give.%0A%0AA%20listed%20company%20cannot.%20A%20hired%20chief%20executive%20holds%20other%20people's%20property%20in%20trust%2C%20and%20handing%20an%20asset%20over%20without%20a%20board%20behind%20him%20is%20not%20generosity%20but%20a%20breach%20of%20duty.%20A%20family%20house%20that%20has%20held%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20no%20such%20problem%3A%20what%20you%20decide%20to%20give%20is%20yours%20to%20give.%20That%20is%20rarer%20than%20it%20sounds%20%E2%80%94%20most%20of%20the%20economy%20has%20lost%20the%20ability.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Schultheiss%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

0given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Oettinger%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

en%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Astra%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)

| Unde | freiberger-pils.de/impressum |

**Subiect:** Ten founding seats on gift.ceo — yours costs nothing

> For Freiberger.
>
> I am writing without knowing what your house has given away, and that is the honest reason for the letter rather than an apology for it. A brewery that has stood in the same town for generations has carried people through bad years — a wage kept paid, a roof fixed, beer at a funeral nobody was billed for. None of it was ever written down anywhere.

[**Deschide în Gmail**](https://mail.google.com/mail/u/0/?view=cm&fs=1&tf=1&to=info%40freiberger-brauhaus.de&su=Ten%20founding%20seats%20on%20gift.ceo%20%E2%80%94%20yours%20costs%20nothing&body=For%20Freiberger.%0A%0AI%20am%20writing%20without%20knowing%20what%20your%20house%20has%20given%20away%2C%20and%20that%20is%20the%20honest%20reason%20for%20the%20letter%20rather%20than%20an%20apology%20for%20it.%20A%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%2C%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%20nobody%20was%20billed%20for.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20ever%20written%20down%20anywhere.%0A%0AYou%20are%20one%20of%20the%20few%20kinds%20of%20company%20that%20can%20actually%20give.%0A%0AA%20listed%20company%20cannot.%20A%20hired%20chief%20executive%20holds%20other%20people's%20property%20in%20trust%2C%20and%20handing%20an%20asset%20over%20without%20a%20board%20behind%20him%20is%20not%20generosity%20but%20a%20breach%20of%20duty.%20A%20family%20house%20that%20has%20held%20the%20same%20town%20for%20generations%20has%20no%20such%20problem%3A%20what%20you%20decide%20to%20give%20is%20yours%20to%20give.%20That%20is%20rarer%20than%20it%20sounds%20%E2%80%94%20most%20of%20the%20economy%20has%20lost%20the%20ability.%0A%0AThat%20is%20why%20I%20am%20writing%20to%20you%2C%20and%20not%20to%20a%20marketing%20department.%0A%0Agift.ceo%20is%20one%20page.%20A%20CEO%20writes%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name%20and%20their%20company's%20name%2C%20a%20single%20thing%3A%20here%20is%20what%20we%20are%20giving.%20A%20tool%2C%20free%20for%20anyone%20who%20needs%20it.%20A%20bonus%20that%20goes%20to%20the%20team.%20Equity%20for%20the%20people%20who%20built%20the%20company.%20A%20day%20of%20your%20time.%20Water%2C%20where%20that%20is%20what%20you%20have.%20No%20foundation%2C%20no%20CSR%20report%2C%20no%20agency%20turning%20it%20into%20a%20campaign.%20Gifts%2C%20numbered%20in%20the%20order%20they%20were%20given%2C%20starting%20at%20%231.%0A%0AThere%20is%20one%20reason%20gift.ceo%20is%20worth%20a%20line%20of%20your%20time.%0A%0AThe%20things%20a%20house%20like%20yours%20gives%20are%20never%20written%20down.%0A%0AA%20brewery%20that%20has%20stood%20in%20the%20same%20town%20through%20two%20wars%20and%20a%20currency%20reform%20has%20carried%20people%20through%20bad%20years%20%E2%80%94%20beer%20at%20a%20funeral%2C%20a%20wage%20kept%20paid%2C%20a%20roof%20fixed%20for%20someone%20who%20could%20not.%20None%20of%20it%20was%20filed.%20gift.ceo%20is%20one%20page%2C%20numbered%2C%20permanent%2C%20where%20one%20such%20thing%20gets%20a%20name%20and%20a%20date.%20Not%20to%20be%20thanked.%20So%20that%20the%20companies%20which%20have%20never%20given%20anything%20have%20to%20look%20at%20a%20page%20where%20the%20ones%20that%20did%20are%20listed.%0A%0AA%20seat%20normally%20costs%2010%2C000%20CHF%2C%20once.%20That%20is%20not%20a%20price%2C%20it%20is%20a%20filter%3A%20only%20a%20CEO%20who%20truly%20intends%20to%20give%20will%20pay%20to%20do%20it%20publicly%2C%20under%20their%20own%20name.%0A%0AThe%20site%20opens%20with%20ten%20founding%20seats%20that%20cost%20nothing.%20They%20pass%20a%20different%20filter%20%E2%80%94%20you%20don't%20pay%2C%20you%20give%20first.%20The%20first%20one%20is%20already%20up%3A%20Cyberbotics%20published%20Webots%2C%20the%20robot%20simulator%20they%20have%20kept%20open%20source%20since%201998.%20One%20condition%3A%20publish%20your%20gift%20within%2030%20days%20and%20the%20seat%20belongs%20to%20Freiberger%20permanently%2C%20whoever%20runs%20it%20later.%0A%0AIf%20you%20want%20one%2C%20reply%20with%20your%20company%20domain%20and%20I%20will%20mark%20it%20before%20you%20sign%20in%2C%20so%20nothing%20ever%20asks%20you%20for%20payment.%0A%0AEight%20of%20the%20ten%20are%20still%20open.%0A%0Agift.ceo%0A%0Agift.ceo.support%40gmail.com%0A%0ASwiss%20made%20%E2%80%94%20Available%20worldwide)
